# SSE R19 fresh-host experiment journal — VM4706

**Log schema:** human-reviewed append-only experiment record, supersede entries rather than silently editing historical facts. **Status:** VM4706 source gates **31/31 Python PASS (Entry 013)**, **Foundry v1.9.0 installed, 60 Solidity files compiled, 155/155 EVM tests PASS (Entry 016)**; native public PoS31340, independently staked validators, P2P and node UI **NOT deployed**.
**UTC date:** 2026-11-19. **Network intended:** new independently operated SSE PoS TEST ETH network, provisional chain ID 31340. **Never** restore legacy compromised chain31339 genesis/keys.

## Entry 001 — routes, DNS, installed prerequisites and external HTTPS verified

**Date/time evidence:** Thu 2026-11-19, GitHub HTTPS `Date` response header **16:23:48 UTC** (remote HTTP response date, not an independent trusted local clock measurement). **Operator action:** user ran the exact ordinary-shell diagnostics from the former planned entry 001; no `sudo`, installer, permission change, new wallet, signature, network peer, chain or host config change.

```sh
printf '\n== addresses ==\n'
ip -br -4 addr
printf '\n== default routing ==\n'
ip -4 route
printf '\n== DNS resolution ==\n'
getent ahostsv4 github.com | head -n 3
printf '\n== installed prerequisites ==\n'
for p in git python3 curl docker besu lighthouse caddy; do
  if command -v "$p" >/dev/null 2>&1; then printf '%s: present\n' "$p"; else printf '%s: absent\n' "$p"; fi
done
printf '\n== HTTPS outbound ==\n'
if command -v curl >/dev/null 2>&1; then curl -I -L --max-time 10 -sS https://github.com/ | head -n 8; fi
```

**Observed output (sanitized, representative details):**
- `lo 127.0.0.1/8`; `eth0 UP 10.50.56.2/24` (**RFC1918**, not Internet-routable); `docker0 DOWN 172.17.0.1/16` (Docker bridge exists, Docker runtime health not tested).
- Default route `via 10.50.56.254 dev eth0 proto dhcp src 10.50.56.2`. 192.168.1.1 and 192.168.1.2 static routes via same private gateway; route table alone does not prove those destinations work.
- `getent ahostsv4 github.com` returned `140.82.121.3` as STREAM/DGRAM/RAW; DNS is available. IP may change; not a bootstrap address.
- Shell executables reported **present**: `git`, `python3`, `curl`, `docker`. **absent**: `besu`, `lighthouse`, `caddy`. This does not prove Docker daemon usable or tool versions compatible.
- HTTPS HEAD to `https://github.com/`: **HTTP/2 200** with HSTS header, response Date `Thu, 19 Nov 2026 16:23:48 GMT`. Outbound HTTPS works to that destination only.
- Exit statuses were not individually captured; command output is internally consistent with successful DNS/HTTPS, but **no claim of full external connectivity**.

**Interpretation:** host has outbound DNS/HTTPS and private IPv4; public IPv4, dynamic IP management, NAT vs CGNAT, inbound devp2p TCP, discv5/CL UDP, external independent peers, signed public genesis, Besu/CL sync and browser-facing HTTPS UI remain **UNTESTED**. The SSH portal may traverse a gateway; it is not usable proof of public peer reachability. We cannot infer a reverse-tunnel requirement or accessibility until checked.

**New manual-to-automation gaps:** network egress and tool inventory should become preflight; Docker daemon/permissions and rootless/least privilege must be validated; missing EL, CL and HTTPS agent install automation; DNS/DDNS/ENR update, firewall operator permission, independent inbound proof. Preserve security-patch debt from entry 000.

**Bugs:** none from this read-only batch. **Blockers:** no public signed network release, no fresh network 31340, no funded independent wallets, no completed installation, no external joining or node UI.

## Entry 002 — requirement captured: automatic valueless test-ETH funding (design and source tests only)

**User requirement:** every newcomer chooses any eligible role combination and gets enough chain-native **valueless TEST ETH** automatically for that role's *actual* on-chain economic prerequisites: Beacon deposit for each validator key (32 test ETH in this PoS design), contract-defined minimum monitor bond, gas for every role's transactions, consumer escrow and monitor/executor incentives based on the chosen lease. No real Ethereum ETH or private mainnet funds. Funds belong to new operator's LOCAL independent wallets, never a shared developer wallet. No admin signup/approval.

**Correctness boundary:** a node may join native P2P and sync with zero balance; only economic actions need money. A role choice neither signs nor spends without owner's approval nor means Beacon/contract activation succeeded. A faucet/claim must be public, objectively bounded and replaceable; no single central funding approval. A finite pool cannot promise infinite test ETH to unrestricted Sybils: document budget, funding and liveness failures; permit independently contributed pools/sponsors or alternative public funders. Do not silently mint fake balances or mark a role funded without finalized on-chain receipts.

**Historical trap:** `tools/sse_pos_live_roles.py` on prior chain31339 directly decrypts one named `faucet` keystore and funds a fixed locally managed set of seven role keys; this cannot serve a real external permissionless network. `tools/sse_pos_validator_deposit.py` verifies a 32-test-ETH deposit but hardcodes chain31339 and requires separate authorized broadcast. No public chain31340 or actual faucet deployed yet.

**Status at recording:** requirement recorded, source funding architecture to be designed and tested; no faucet, transfers, independent wallet provisioning, sponsored transactions, or Beacon deposits executed.

## Entry 005 — observed Docker socket permissions and package origins

**Date:** 2026-11-19 (exact terminal time not captured); **source:** user's VM4706 terminal. **Commands executed**, as prepared in planned Entry 005: `id -nG`; `stat -c '%A %U:%G %n' /var/run/docker.sock 2>&1`; `systemctl is-active docker 2>&1`; `apt-cache policy docker.io tailscale cloud-init | head -n 80`; test for `/var/run/reboot-required`. No privilege escalation or host mutation.

**Results:**
- Effective groups: `ayoubakrvm4706 adm cdrom sudo dip video plugdev`. User is NOT in `docker`.
- Docker socket: `srw-rw---- root:docker /var/run/docker.sock` (0660), inaccessible to current user.
- `systemctl is-active docker` -> `active`. **Root cause CONFIRMED** for Entry 004 `docker info` failure (exit 1): running Docker daemon but nonmember of socket's privileged group. No working root-authorized Docker API command yet observed.
- Ubuntu `docker.io`: installed and candidate `29.1.3-0ubuntu3~24.04.2`, Ubuntu noble-updates/noble-security/universe.
- `tailscale`: installed `1.98.4`; candidate `1.104.7` from **independent third-party** `https://pkgs.tailscale.com/stable/ubuntu`. This is inherited VM software, NOT SSE permissionless network membership or a required mesh. Do NOT run `tailscale up` or change portal management networking. The `cloud-init` policy was **cut off by the 80-line `head` truncation** and cannot be inferred.
- `no-reboot-required-marker` -> file absent at inspection; previous pending security updates still not applied; no guarantee of current patched kernel.

**Bug ledger:** `DOCKER_SOCKET_PERMISSION_DENIED` diagnosed without installing/removing anything. No change applied, no upgrade, no Docker-group membership, no daemon API call, no chain/genesis/wallet/validator/faucet, no external P2P or public UI. The final external role installer must not grant permanent root-equivalent access to untrusted node owners as normal enrollment. Container workload runtime choice remains pending: rootless Docker is a candidate, but Ubuntu 24.04 AppArmor/user namespaces and cgroup-v2 resource constraints must be reviewed/tested. The source checkout/tests are independent of Docker and can run unprivileged.

**Next planned entry:** scoped read-only `sudo -n docker info` (only if sudo permitted) and check rootless prerequisites. No root credential, SSH private key, package change or TCP/UDP service exposure. A user choosing a role does not confer privileged host or chain admission.

## Entry 006 — Docker daemon verified; rootless helpers absent (OBSERVED)

**UTC date:** 2026-11-19. **Evidence:** operator-provided VM4706 output of the **planned Entry 006** block. No container launched, no package installed, no user permission or firewall changed.

- `sudo -n docker info --format 'server={{.ServerVersion}} driver={{.Driver}} cgroup={{.CgroupVersion}}'`: output **`server=29.1.3 driver=overlayfs cgroup=2`**; captured `sudo_docker_info_exit=0`. This confirms the OS Docker service is working when invoked with allowed sudo. It DOES NOT prove rootless isolation or actual provider compute delivery.
- Executables `newuidmap`, `newgidmap`, `rootlesskit`, `dockerd-rootless-setuptool.sh`: all **absent**. Need to install/review dependencies before a real rootless runtime test.
- `/sys/fs/cgroup/cgroup.controllers` exists: **cgroup v2 = yes**. The requested limits and delegation are NOT tested.
- `/sys/module/apparmor/parameters/enabled`: **`Y`**. This is AppArmor availability, not a proof that the planned rootless container launch is permitted by profiles.
- Subordinate ID mappings: current account has **65536 IDs** each in readable `/etc/subuid` and `/etc/subgid`. This is a prerequisite, not an active rootless daemon.
- `sudo -n` worked for this Docker read query. Do not infer general passwordless root permission or that arbitrary external nodes have such privilege.
- No BLS/EOA wallet created, funded or staked; no public PoS network launched, no external TCP/UDP reachability, no per-node HTTPS UI. Tailscale not used or changed.
- **Root cause and intended fix:** Entry 004 access denial is correct Unix socket access control; short-term owner-authorized Docker inspection uses scoped sudo; long-term provider workload runner should use reviewed rootless container engine or other least-privilege sandbox with measured resource limits. Do not add every joining user to privileged `docker` group. No fix installed yet.

**Source work next:** `scripts/r19_verify_fresh_host.sh` (GitHub PR #35) pins exact commit SHA, runs Python unit tests and local inventory without Docker privileges. Its runtime is unverified until executed on VM4706. The **private GitHub repository** still requires a developer-owned *read-only deploy key* for this *temporary development trial*. This is a manual SOURCE access step only; **external protocol nodes must later download public, independently mirrored and verified release artifacts with no GitHub credentials, account invitation or central joining authority.**

## Entry 011 — public network preflight FAILURES, causes and GitHub-only fixes

**Date:** 2026-11-19. **Evidence type:** actual VM4706 source unit-test failure, followed by SOURCE code review and GitHub commits; **regression fixes not yet rerun on VM4706**.

**Actual operator command** (verified pinned commit `1e8c1e7fda61c4b4cafa0eac83cac80d355e2ee5`):
```bash
(
  set -e
  cd "$HOME/sse-research"
  EXPECTED_SHA="1e8c1e7fda61c4b4cafa0eac83cac80d355e2ee5"
  test "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$EXPECTED_SHA" || { echo "ERROR: Unexpected Git revision"; exit 1; }
  echo "== Public permissionless network tests =="
  python3 -B -m unittest discover -s tests -p 'test_sse_public_*.py' -v
  echo "== Clean architecture integrity tests =="
  python3 -B -m unittest discover -s tests -p 'test_sse_mainline_integrity.py' -v
  echo "== Install pinned Foundry source dependencies =="
  git submodule update --init --recursive
  echo "== Dependency revisions =="
  git submodule status
  echo "== Completed =="
)
```

**Actual test result:** **10 test methods executed, 8 passed and 2 failed**, 0.001s. The Python exit was nonzero and `set -e` stopped the subshell. Thus mainline-integrity tests, `git submodule update`, and submodule SHA reporting **DID NOT RUN**. No software dependencies installed or code modified on VM4706 in this action.

**Failed assertions:**
1. `test_admin_invite_is_not_eligibility_for_any_role`, line 90: `AssertionError: PublicManifestError not raised`. The subcase deletes `manifest["permissionlessRoles"]["monitor"]`; `fixture()` passed the same global mutable `ROLE_RULES` dictionary object to the manifest. The delete thus altered the *validator's own expected policy*. Comparing the mutated manifest to the mutated global wrongly succeeded. **Security property violated:** canonical protocol role eligibility should not be changeable through test/manifest data.
2. `test_wont_accept_overlay_lab_or_nonpublic_fqdn`, subTest `host='10.50.56.13'`, line 74: `AssertionError: PublicManifestError not raised`. Source `DNS` regex accepted dotted all-numeric labels as domain names, with no explicit IP-literal exclusion. **Security property violated:** IP-literal acceptance when public bootnode requires DNS discovery; may lead to hardcoded private addresses.

**Root-cause review:** both derived directly from `tools/sse_public_network_preflight.py` and `tests/test_sse_public_network_preflight.py` at exact observed checkout. Fixes applied **ONLY through GitHub** in draft PR #35:
- `1dee0091f460bf39fdc8e2e363ea8d35d8b57213`: freeze `ROLE_RULES` behind a read-only `MappingProxyType`, validate JSON role-policy object type explicitly and reject numeric dotted address literals in `_fqdn` (including public and private numeric hosts).
- `50645704658644365e5fd4f256edb91feabd81b7`: make each fixture obtain `dict(ROLE_RULES)` (independent copy); add 3 security regression methods asserting policy immutability and independence, private/public/nonsensical numeric IP-literal rejection in enode and genesis-mirror HTTPS URL.
- These commits are **source edits only**. Unit suite expected to contain **13** methods after these fixes; exact results remain **UNVERIFIED** until VM4706 fetches and runs the changed commit. Do not claim PASS before observing output.

**Next action:** fetch updated existing review branch with the VM's already validated read-only deploy key; detach to **the newly verified immutable HEAD SHA**; run Python tests for the original 13 fresh-host cases, 13 public-preflight cases and mainline integrity. If all pass, run pinned public Git submodule checkout and report exact submodule SHAs. No `pip`, forge, Docker, Tailscale, Besu, Lighthouse, funder or validator launch at this stage.

**Automation requirements exposed:** immutable role policy must be inherent in code (not test order), strict IP/DNS validation must be protected with adversarial tests, test result gating before other installation steps, and failing commands logged/retained. These failures are real security-test findings, not live network faults; no actual public PoS network is currently deployed.

**Root cause:** GitHub release artifact checksum SHA-256 **MATCHED** the official pinned release asset. The installer then mistakenly required `tar -tzf` archive listing to have exactly **4 entries**. The real checked archive has a non-four listing (the exact names/count were not printed, so **must not invent them**); that count is **not** a security invariant. The correctly downloaded, verified, official release was rejected before extraction/installation. The `trap` cleanup should remove staging directories; user has not independently inspected this effect. No Foundry EVM/compile tests run; no live network touched.

**Fix committed exclusively through GitHub** at `cd762a894b703355557519e18318e4e9b2b4e1d0`, `scripts/r19_install_foundry.sh`:
- Retain exact pinned release `v1.9.0` and expected `8d17599b42e3139f1a9c171be3798c4ed8e743ae17b84cc3435e57bf2a51ae99` archive digest.
- Remove the invalid *total archive entry count* assumption. Parse the tar listing successfully; require exactly one archive entry for each *specific* needed tool `forge, cast, anvil, chisel`, optionally prefixed `./`.
- Extract **only those four specifically allowlisted paths** and ignore surprise metadata/extras. Reject absent or duplicated required tool paths, then require regular non-symlink files, chmod user-owned install, execute `forge --version` before final `mv`. Do NOT extract arbitrary untrusted tar paths.
- No installer changes were made directly on VM4706. All new fixes remain SOURCE until exact new PR SHA is fetched and tested there.

**Next checkpoint:** fetch the immutable new PR #35 head, inspect/pin exact SHA, execute `bash -n scripts/r19_install_foundry.sh`, then installer and focused/full `forge` EVM tests with exact tools. ## Entry 036 — permissionless expiration and no-reroll of unconfirmed variable committees, 5 new EVM tests (SOURCE ONLY)

**Date:** 2026-11-20, after verified Entry 035 result. All changes are GitHub-only on **draft PR #35**; **NO NEW VM4706 execution** for these commits. These additions respond to the risk that in a variable-sized committee a single absent monitor can hold an otherwise funded lease forever or that a consumer can request an unfair reroll when its chosen monitors do not confirm.

**Actual source modifications not yet VM-tested:**
- `610113cc55d4ce9a06aa6f1cabf34ed82c647ed1`: `contracts/SSEAdaptiveVoteLedger.sol` extends frozen `FreezeTerms` with an explicit 1-hour to 7-day bounded `confirmationTimeoutSeconds`, freezes `Committee.confirmationDeadline` on first on-chain selection (never extendable by partial confirmations), rejects confirmations after the deadline, and provides permissionless `expireUnconfirmed(leaseId)` **only** after the immutable deadline while any seats remain unconfirmed. Once expired it cannot receive late confirmations, become Ready, open an epoch or be re-frozen under the same lease identifier. Emits `CommitteeConfirmationExpired`; does **not** create payout authority, refund funds, slash anyone, rotate standbys or fake votes.
- `ef03d909e6c76f058a1cc628b61a3d856b56192f`: extend `foundry/test/SSEAdaptiveVoteLedger.t.sol` with real EVM unit fixture cases for outsider-triggered deadline expiry, early expiration refusal and no reroll, confirmed committees never cancel, too short/zero/too long timeout refusal. All old fixture policies now pass an explicit one-hour timeout. `4374d87182c55c4e422642ebd90ee2ef75b04865`: explicitly cast timeout test literals to uint64.
- `0754578988ebaa9be8d11c7fc373a66c7adf7803`: add two more adversarial EVM unit tests for **late confirmations forbidden even without anyone calling expiry** and for EIP712 signature refusal after a chain-ID change. Total **21 new V2 tests** in current source, **not yet run at this revision**; original 16/16 baseline plus full 181/181 at earlier `b64aa72` are documented as previous actual run.
- `docs/R22_ADAPTIVE_VOTE_LEDGER.md` updated with actual earlier PASS and precise unexecuted scope. **No GitHub Actions, VM source edits, independent physical monitors, actual off-chain SLA observations or new contract deployment**.

**Remaining blockers:** cannot claim dynamic end-to-end SSE until an objectively verified permissionless monitor registry and selection proof are enforced by immutable settlement, real on-chain lease escrow and monitor rewards exist, timeout triggers safe consumer/provider funds handling, real provider EIP712/consumer-delivery data is checked, and the four world-soundness counterexamples are addressed. The V2 ledger itself has no balances and cannot refund an expired assignment. ## Entry 038 — R24 enforce actually funded native TEST ETH monitor reporting incentives and conservation accounting (SOURCE ONLY, NOT VM TESTED)

It would be unsafe to advertise report incentives as funded. Such funding can be forged by a committee/settlement caller until the ledger itself checks ETH received.

- `d686fff1d72d2d74d795c8b6d2a568670d3dfcf3`: make `freezeCommittee` `payable onlySettlement`, REQUIRE `msg.value==p.monitorBudget`, exact balance equal `selectedSeats.length * payableEpochs * perMonitorEpochReward`; record actual reserve and per-report reward in committee state. `submitSignedVote`/`submitVoteDirect` credit **one paid report per selected monitor and epoch** (even dissenting valid reports); never reward mere confirmation. Early no-show expiration and only-settlement close when every opened epoch sealed turn unspent monitoring reserve into **consumer-specific pull-payment credits**, not arbitrarily routed funds; `withdraw` is a reentry-guarded, effects-before-external-call claim to `msg.sender` only. `openEpoch` now requires sequential indices and no more epochs than native funds reserved. - `58c116e701e6016d175e7f90329c7e5ef682975d`: add `totalUnspentReserves`, `totalWithdrawableCredits` counters, emit per-seat `SeatSelected` access-control event, and maintain the conservation relation `address(ledger).balance == totalUnspentReserves + totalWithdrawableCredits` barring externally forcibly sent ETH.

## Append-entry policy for ALL subsequent work

Each future entry must include:
1. UTC timestamp, VM/host and separately verified owner/infrastructure fault domain.
2. Exact source Git commit SHA / PR and dependency version lock; chosen roles (requested versus actually eligible/active).
3. **Exact commands in order** with tested exit codes, redacted terminal output, package/OS versions, effects on local resources, ports and firewalls.
4. Before/after observed condition; measured peer count/finality/effective DNS/ENR/TLS visibility and precise evidence artifact hashes when available.
5. Any bug: **error**, reproducible trigger, root-cause hypothesis vs demonstrated root cause, minimal fix commit/PR, focused regression test and rerun result, rollback or recovery effects.
6. Any manual step that the final role-only installer must eliminate; conversion into source-controlled machine automation and reinstallation on a truly fresh external host.
7. Whether the result is **SOURCE**, **LAB OBSERVED**, **INDEPENDENT EXTERNAL LIVE** or **NOT TESTED**. No source/unit tests stand in for live physical independence.
8. Safety: do **not** publish private keys, passwords, TLS certificate keys, SSH deploy keys, raw authentication headers, VM cloud credentials, secret-containing bootstrap output, raw publicly sensitive services or unrestricted private logs to the private repo, issues or chat. Record only sanitized summaries and cryptographic hashes of nonsecret evidence.

## Entry 006 reference commands — executed by operator

```sh
printf '\n== Docker daemon with scoped sudo, no changes ==\n'
sudo -n docker info --format 'server={{.ServerVersion}} driver={{.Driver}} cgroup={{.CgroupVersion}}' 2>&1
printf 'sudo_docker_info_exit=%s\n' "$?"
printf '\n== rootless prerequisites ==\n'
for p in newuidmap newgidmap rootlesskit dockerd-rootless-setuptool.sh; do
  if command -v "$p" >/dev/null 2>&1; then printf '%s: present\n' "$p"; else printf '%s: absent\n' "$p"; fi
done
printf 'cgroup_v2='
if [ -f /sys/fs/cgroup/cgroup.controllers ]; then echo yes; else echo no; fi
printf 'apparmor='
if [ -f /sys/module/apparmor/parameters/enabled ]; then cat /sys/module/apparmor/parameters/enabled; else echo unknown; fi
printf '\n== subordinate namespace mapping counts ==\n'
for p in /etc/subuid /etc/subgid; do
  if [ -r "$p" ]; then awk -F: -v u="$(id -un)" '$1==u {print FILENAME ": " $3 " IDs"}' "$p"; else echo "$p not readable"; fi
done
```

`sudo -n` will fail (without a password prompt) if password-free sudo is not authorized. It does not change permissions or the Docker service. Do not add user to Docker group or weaken AppArmor to make rootless tooling work until a reviewed installation strategy is approved.
